{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013WFWYv67LJ5RwTiMrjWZrK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * W4b (ADR-0013 / BILLING-CRITICAL-002): `billing.plans` becomes the ONLY plan\n5\t * catalogue, and the money `admin.plan_definitions` kept in jsonb becomes rows.\n6\t *\n7\t * Two catalogues existed. Every runtime path — create-subscription,\n8\t * change-plan, the billing scheduler, the provisioning handler — resolves\n9\t * `billing.plans`; `admin.plan_definitions` carried its own ids that nothing\n10\t * ever resolved, its own `stripeProductId` / `stripePriceIds` (a Stripe object\n11\t * has one owner, and two writable homes means two services can mint a product\n12\t * for the same plan), and a per-cycle price matrix inside `jsonb` where no\n13\t * CHECK could reach a negative price or a `discountPercent` of 400.\n14\t *\n15\t * WHAT MOVES:\n16\t *   - the presentation and lifecycle columns (`code`, descriptions,\n17\t *     `visibility`, `is_recommended`, trial/grace days, upgrade/downgrade\n18\t *     copy, icon/color/badge) onto `billing.plans`;\n19\t *   - the per-cycle price matrix into `billing.plan_cycle_prices`, one row per\n20\t *     (plan, cycle) with `numeric(19,4)` prices and `discount_percent` CHECKed\n21\t *     into [0, 100];\n22\t *   - `features.addOns[]` into `billing.plan_add_ons`, because an add-on price\n23\t *     was money nested two levels inside a features blob;\n24\t *   - `features`' three named sets into `billing.plans.features`, widening it\n25\t *     from a flat `string[]`.\n26\t *\n27\t * IDENTITY: `billing.plans.name` is already UNIQUE — it is the catalogue's own\n28\t * business key — so an admin definition whose name matches a billing plan\n29\t * UPDATES that plan (the operator's authored copy lands on the row that is\n30\t * actually used), and one whose name is new is INSERTED keeping its own id. No\n31\t * id is remapped, no existing plan is duplicated, and nothing authored is\n32\t * discarded. `admin.plan_definitions.pricing` also seeds the cycle rows for\n33\t * plans that had none.\n34\t *\n35\t * NOT touched: `billing.plans.pricing`, the flat per-unit rate card a\n36\t * subscription snapshots at signup. It is the same shape as\n37\t * `billing.subscriptions.pricing` and normalising one without the other would\n38\t * split the snapshot; both are BILLING-CRITICAL-003's, and both stay in the\n39\t * money-in-jsonb ratchet until then.\n40\t *\n41\t * Ordering: `SCHEMA_REGISTRY` runs billing (slot 8) before admin (slot 11), so\n42\t * this merge always precedes the admin-side drop, which re-verifies it.\n43\t */\n44\texport class MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n45\t  name = 'MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000';\n46\t\n47\t  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n48\t    await queryRunner.query(\n49\t      `DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE \"billing\".\"plans_visibility_enum\" AS ENUM('public', 'private', 'deprecated'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;`,\n50\t    );\n51\t\n52\t    for (const [column, type] of [\n53\t      ['code', 'character varying(100)'],\n54\t      ['description', 'text'],\n55\t      ['short_description', 'text'],\n56\t      ['is_recommended', 'boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false'],\n57\t      ['trial_days', 'integer'],\n58\t      ['grace_period_days', 'integer'],\n59\t      ['upgrade_message', 'text'],\n60\t      ['downgrade_warning', 'text'],\n61\t      ['icon', 'character varying(64)'],\n62\t      ['color', 'character varying(32)'],\n63\t      ['badge', 'character varying(64)'],\n64\t    ] as const) {\n65\t      await queryRunner.query(\n66\t        `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"${column}\" ${type}`,\n67\t      );\n68\t    }\n69\t    await queryRunner.query(\n70\t      `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"visibility\" \"billing\".\"plans_visibility_enum\" NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`,\n71\t    );\n72\t\n73\t    // `code` is the operator-facing catalogue key. Partial UNIQUE so the plans\n74\t    // that predate the merge (which have none) do not all collide on NULL.\n75\t    await queryRunner.query(\n76\t      `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"UQ_billing_plans_code\" ON \"billing\".\"plans\" (\"code\") WHERE \"code\" IS NOT NULL`,\n77\t    );\n78\t    await queryRunner.query(\n79\t      `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"IDX_billing_plans_visibility\" ON \"billing\".\"plans\" (\"visibility\")`,\n80\t    );\n81\t\n82\t    // `features` widens from a flat array to the three named sets. Guarded by\n83\t    // an information_schema probe so a replay after a partial failure is safe.\n84\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n85\t      DO $$\n86\t      BEGIN\n87\t        IF EXISTS (\n88\t          SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns\n89\t           WHERE table_schema = 'billing' AND table_name = 'plans' AND column_name = 'features'\n90\t        ) THEN\n91\t          UPDATE \"billing\".\"plans\"\n92\t             SET \"features\" = jsonb_build_object(\n93\t                   'coreFeatures', COALESCE(\"features\", '[]'::jsonb),\n94\t                   'advancedFeatures', '[]'::jsonb,\n95\t                   'premiumFeatures', '[]'::jsonb\n96\t                 )\n97\t           WHERE jsonb_typeof(\"features\") = 'array';\n98\t        END IF;\n99\t      END $$;\n100\t    `);\n101\t\n102\t    // The column default still produced a flat array for new rows.\n103\t    await queryRunner.query(\n104\t      `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ALTER COLUMN \"features\" SET DEFAULT '{\"coreFeatures\":[],\"advancedFeatures\":[],\"premiumFeatures\":[]}'::jsonb`,\n105\t    );\n106\t\n107\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n108\t      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"billing\".\"plan_cycle_prices\" (\n109\t        \"id\" uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n110\t        \"plan_id\" uuid NOT NULL,\n111\t        \"billing_cycle\" \"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\" NOT NULL,\n112\t        \"base_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL,\n113\t        \"per_user_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n114\t        \"per_farm_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n115\t        \"per_module_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n116\t        \"discount_percent\" numeric(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n117\t        CONSTRAINT \"PK_billing_plan_cycle_prices\" PRIMARY KEY (\"id\"),\n118\t        CONSTRAINT \"FK_billing_plan_cycle_prices_plan\"\n119\t          FOREIGN KEY (\"plan_id\") REFERENCES \"billing\".\"plans\"(\"id\") ON DELETE CASCADE,\n120\t        CONSTRAINT \"UQ_billing_plan_cycle_prices_cycle\" UNIQUE (\"plan_id\", \"billing_cycle\"),\n121\t        CONSTRAINT (\"id\") DO NOTHING\n255\t    `);\n256\t\n257\t    // The per-cycle matrix, one row per cycle the definition prices.\n258\t    for (const [jsonKey, cycle] of [\n259\t      ['monthly', \"per_module_price\", \"discount_percent\"\n269\t        )\n270\t        SELECT\n271\t          p.\"id\",\n272\t          $2::\"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\",\n273\t          COALESCE((d.\"pricing\"->$1->>'basePrice')::numeric, 0),\n274\t          COALESCE((d.\"pricing\"->$1->>'perUserPrice')::numeric, 0),\n275\t          COALESCE((d.\"pricing\"->$1->>'perFarmPrice')::numeric, 0),\n276\t          COALESCE((d.\"pricing\"->$1->>'perModulePrice')::numeric, 0),\n277\t          COALESCE((d.\"pricing\"->$1->>'discountPercent')::numeric, 0)\n278\t        FROM \"admin\".\"plan_definitions\" d\n279\t        JOIN \"billing\".\"plans\" p ON p.\"name\" = d.\"name\"\n280\t        WHERE d.\"pricing\" ? $1\n281\t        ON CONFLICT (\"plan_id\", \"billing_cycle\") DO NOTHING\n282\t        `,\n283\t        [jsonKey, cycle],\n284\t      );\n285\t    }\n286\t\n287\t    await SELECT\n292\t        p.\"id\",\n293\t        add_on->>'code',\n294\t        add_on->>'name',\n295\t        add_on->>'description',\n296\t        (add_on->>'price')::numeric,\n297\t        (add_on->>'billingCycle')::\"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\"\n298\t      FROM \"admin\".\"plan_definitions\" d\n299\t      JOIN \"billing\".\"plans\" p ON p.\"name\" = d.\"name\"\n300\t      CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(d.\"features\"->'addOns', '[]'::jsonb)) AS add_on\n301\t      ON CONFLICT (\"plan_id\", \"code\") DO NOTHING\n302\t    `);\n303\t\n304\t    // Every definition must have landed somewhere, and every add-on it priced\n305\t    // must be a row — otherwise the admin-side drop would destroy the only copy.\n306\t    const missing = (await queryRunner.query(`\n307\t      SELECT count(*)::text AS missing\n308\t        FROM \"admin\".\"plan_definitions\" d\n309\t       WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM \"billing\".\"plans\" p WHERE p.\"name\" = d.\"name\")\n310\t    `)) as Array<{ missing: string }>;\n311\t    if ((missing[0]?.missing ?? '0') !== '0') {\n312\t      throw new Error(\n313\t        `MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000: ${missing[0]?.missing} admin.plan_definitions rows did not merge into billing.plans. Resolve the source rows before re-running.`,\n314\t      );\n315\t    }\n316\t  }\n317\t\n318\t  /**\n319\t   * A plan that predates the merge has no per-cycle rows. Seed its monthly\n320\t   * cycle from the flat rate card it already carries, so every plan can be\n321\t   * priced from the same place; the other cycles are an operator decision, not\n322\t   * something a migration should invent.\n323\t   */\n324\t  private async seedCyclePricesForUnmatchedPlans(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n325\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n326\t      INSERT INTO \"billing\".\"plan_cycle_prices\" (\n327\t        \"plan_id\", \"billing_cycle\", \"base_price\", \"per_user_price\",\n328\t        \"per_farm_price\", \"per_module_price\", \"discount_percent\"\n329\t      )\n330\t      SELECT\n331\t        p.\"id\",\n332\t        'monthly'::\"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\",\n333\t        COALESCE((p.\"pricing\"->>'basePrice')::numeric, p.\"base_price\"),\n334\t        COALESCE((p.\"pricing\"->>'perUserPrice')::numeric, 0),\n335\t        COALESCE((p.\"pricing\"->>'perFarmPrice')::numeric, 0),\n336\t        0,\n337\t        0\n338\t      FROM \"billing\".\"plans\" p\n339\t      WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n340\t        SELECT 1 FROM \"billing\".\"plan_cycle_prices\" c WHERE c.\"plan_id\" = p.\"id\"\n341\t      )\n342\t      ON CONFLICT (\"plan_id\", \"billing_cycle\") DO NOTHING\n343\t    `);\n344\t  }\n345\t\n346\t  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n347\t    // DESTRUCTIVE: down()-only rollback of MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000 — drops the plan_add_ons table this same migration created; Every runtime path — create-subscription,\n * change-plan, the billing scheduler, the provisioning handler — resolves\n * `billing.plans`; `admin.plan_definitions` carried its own ids that nothing\n * ever resolved, its own `stripeProductId` / `stripePriceIds` (a Stripe object\n * has one owner, and two writable homes means two services can mint a product\n * for the same plan), and a per-cycle price matrix inside `jsonb` where no\n * CHECK could reach a negative price or a `discountPercent` of 400.\n *\n * WHAT MOVES:\n *   - the presentation and lifecycle columns (`code`, descriptions,\n *     `visibility`, `is_recommended`, trial/grace days, upgrade/downgrade\n *     copy, icon/color/badge) onto `billing.plans`;\n *   - the per-cycle price matrix into `billing.plan_cycle_prices`, one row per\n *     (plan, cycle) with `numeric(19,4)` prices and `discount_percent` CHECKed\n *     into [0, 100];\n *   - `features.addOns[]` into `billing.plan_add_ons`, because an add-on price\n *     was money nested two levels inside a features blob;\n *   - `features`' three named sets into `billing.plans.features`, widening it\n *     from a flat `string[]`.\n *\n * IDENTITY: `billing.plans.name` is already UNIQUE — it is the catalogue's own\n * business key — so an admin definition whose name matches a billing plan\n * UPDATES that plan (the operator's authored copy lands on the row that is\n * actually used), and one whose name is new is INSERTED keeping its own id. No\n * id is remapped, no existing plan is duplicated, and nothing authored is\n * discarded. `admin.plan_definitions.pricing` also seeds the cycle rows for\n * plans that had none.\n *\n * NOT touched: `billing.plans.pricing`, the flat per-unit rate card a\n * subscription snapshots at signup. It is the same shape as\n * `billing.subscriptions.pricing` and normalising one without the other would\n * split the snapshot; both are BILLING-CRITICAL-003's, and both stay in the\n * money-in-jsonb ratchet until then.\n *\n * Ordering: `SCHEMA_REGISTRY` runs billing (slot 8) before admin (slot 11), so\n * this merge always precedes the admin-side drop, which re-verifies it.\n */\nexport class MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n  name = 'MergePlanCatalogue1802500000000';\n\n  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `DO $$ BEGIN CREATE TYPE \"billing\".\"plans_visibility_enum\" AS ENUM('public', 'private', 'deprecated'); EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$;`,\n    );\n\n    for (const [column, type] of [\n      ['code', 'character varying(100)'],\n      ['description', 'text'],\n      ['short_description', 'text'],\n      ['is_recommended', 'boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false'],\n      ['trial_days', 'integer'],\n      ['grace_period_days', 'integer'],\n      ['upgrade_message', 'text'],\n      ['downgrade_warning', 'text'],\n      ['icon', 'character varying(64)'],\n      ['color', 'character varying(32)'],\n      ['badge', 'character varying(64)'],\n    ] as const) {\n      await queryRunner.query(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"${column}\" ${type}`,\n      );\n    }\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"visibility\" \"billing\".\"plans_visibility_enum\" NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`,\n    );\n\n    // `code` is the operator-facing catalogue key. Partial UNIQUE so the plans\n    // that predate the merge (which have none) do not all collide on NULL.\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"UQ_billing_plans_code\" ON \"billing\".\"plans\" (\"code\") WHERE \"code\" IS NOT NULL`,\n    );\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"IDX_billing_plans_visibility\" ON \"billing\".\"plans\" (\"visibility\")`,\n    );\n\n    // `features` widens from a flat array to the three named sets. Guarded by\n    // an information_schema probe so a replay after a partial failure is safe.\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      DO $$\n      BEGIN\n        IF EXISTS (\n          SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns\n           WHERE table_schema = 'billing' AND table_name = 'plans' AND column_name = 'features'\n        ) THEN\n          UPDATE \"billing\".\"plans\"\n             SET \"features\" = jsonb_build_object(\n                   'coreFeatures', COALESCE(\"features\", '[]'::jsonb),\n                   'advancedFeatures', '[]'::jsonb,\n                   'premiumFeatures', '[]'::jsonb\n                 )\n           WHERE jsonb_typeof(\"features\") = 'array';\n        END IF;\n      END $$;\n    `);\n\n    // The column default still produced a flat array for new rows.\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `ALTER TABLE \"billing\".\"plans\" ALTER COLUMN \"features\" SET DEFAULT '{\"coreFeatures\":[],\"advancedFeatures\":[],\"premiumFeatures\":[]}'::jsonb`,\n    );\n\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"billing\".\"plan_cycle_prices\" (\n        \"id\" uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n        \"plan_id\" uuid NOT NULL,\n        \"billing_cycle\" \"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\" NOT NULL,\n        \"base_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL,\n        \"per_user_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        \"per_farm_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        \"per_module_price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        \"discount_percent\" numeric(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        CONSTRAINT \"PK_billing_plan_cycle_prices\" PRIMARY KEY (\"id\"),\n        CONSTRAINT \"FK_billing_plan_cycle_prices_plan\"\n          FOREIGN KEY (\"plan_id\") REFERENCES \"billing\".\"plans\"(\"id\") ON DELETE CASCADE,\n        CONSTRAINT \"UQ_billing_plan_cycle_prices_cycle\" UNIQUE (\"plan_id\", \"billing_cycle\"),\n        CONSTRAINT \"CHK_billing_plan_cycle_prices_amounts\" CHECK (\n          \"base_price\" >= 0 AND \"per_user_price\" >= 0\n          AND \"per_farm_price\" >= 0 AND \"per_module_price\" >= 0\n        ),\n        CONSTRAINT \"CHK_billing_plan_cycle_prices_discount\"\n          CHECK (\"discount_percent\" >= 0 AND \"discount_percent\" <= 100)\n      )\n    `);\n\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"billing\".\"plan_add_ons\" (\n        \"id\" uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n        \"plan_id\" uuid NOT NULL,\n        \"code\" character varying(100) NOT NULL,\n        \"name\" character varying(255) NOT NULL,\n        \"description\" text,\n        \"price\" numeric(19,4) NOT NULL,\n        \"billing_cycle\" \"billing\".\"plans_billing_cycle_enum\" NOT NULL,\n        CONSTRAINT \"PK_billing_plan_add_ons\" PRIMARY KEY (\"id\"),\n        CONSTRAINT \"FK_billing_plan_add_ons_plan\"\n          FOREIGN KEY (\"plan_id\") REFERENCES \"billing\".\"plans\"(\"id\") ON DELETE CASCADE,\n        CONSTRAINT \"UQ_billing_plan_add_ons_code\" UNIQUE (\"plan_id\", \"code\"),\n        CONSTRAINT \"CHK_billing_plan_add_ons_price\" CHECK (\"price\" >= 0)\n      )\n    `);\n\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"IDX_billing_plan_cycle_prices_plan\" ON \"billing\".\"plan_cycle_prices\" (\"plan_id\")`,\n    );\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"IDX_billing_plan_add_ons_plan\" ON \"billing\".\"plan_add_ons\" (\"plan_id\")`,\n    );\n\n    await this.mergeFromAdmin(queryRunner);\n    await this.seedCyclePricesForUnmatchedPlans(queryRunner);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Fold `admin.plan_definitions` into `billing.plans`.\n   *\n   * Matching is by `name`, the catalogue's own UNIQUE business key: a match\n   * UPDATES the live plan with the operator's authored copy, a miss INSERTS\n   * the definition keeping its id. Both branches then expand the jsonb price\n   * matrix and the add-ons into rows; a value that cannot be represented\n   * exactly violates a CHECK and aborts, rather than being rounded.\n   */\n  private async mergeFromAdmin(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    const presence = (await queryRunner.query(\n      `SELECT to_regclass('admin.plan_definitions') IS NOT NULL AS present`,\n    )) as Array<{ present: boolean }>;\n    if (!presence[0]?.present) return;\n\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      UPDATE \"billing\".\"plans\" p\n         SET \"code\" = COALESCE(p.\"code\", d.\"code\"),\n             \"description\" = COALESCE(p.\"description\", d.\"description\"),\n             \"short_description\" = COALESCE(p.\"short_description\", d.\"shortDescription\"),\n             \"visibility\" = d.\"visibility\"::text::\"billing\".\"plans_visibility_enum\",\n             \"is_recommended\" = d.\"isRecommended\",\n             \"trial_days\" = COALESCE(p.\"trial_days\", d.\"trialDays\"),\n             \"grace_period_days\" = COALESCE(p.\"grace_period_days\", d.\"gracePeriodDays\"),\n             \"upgrade_message\" = COALESCE(p.\"upgrade_message\", d.\"upgradeMessage\"),\n             \"downgrade_warning\" = COALESCE(p.\"downgrade_warning\", d.\"downgradeWarning\"),\n             \"icon\" = COALESCE(p.\"icon\", d.\"icon\"),\n             \"color\" = COALESCE(p.\"color\", d.\"color\"),\n             \"badge\" = COALESCE(p.\"badge\", d.\"badge\"),\n             \"stripe_product_id\" = COALESCE(p.\"stripe_product_id\", d.\"stripeProductId\"),\n             \"stripe_price_ids\" = COALESCE(p.\"stripe_price_ids\", d.\"stripePriceIds\"),\n             \"sort_order\" = d.\"sortOrder\",\n             \"features\" = jsonb_build_object(\n               'coreFeatures', COALESCE(d.\"features\"->'coreFeatures', p.\"features\"->'coreFeatures', '[]'::jsonb),\n               'advancedFeatures', COALESCE(d.\"features\"->'advancedFeatures', '[]'::jsonb),\n               'premiumFeatures', COALESCE(d.\"features\"->'premiumFeatures', '[]'::jsonb)\n             ),\n             \"updatedAt\" =
